{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_012uCCcgQxFxsD3tDyQ5sAaF","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→%%\n     2→%% %CopyrightBegin%\n     3→%%\n     4→%% Copyright Ericsson AB 2004-2025. All Rights Reserved.\n     5→%%\n     6→%% Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n     7→%% you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n     8→%% You may obtain a copy of the License at\n     9→%%\n    10→%%     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n    11→%%\n    12→%% Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n    13→%% distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n    14→%% WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n    15→%% See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n    16→%% limitations under the License.\n    17→%%\n    18→%% %CopyrightEnd%\n    19→%%\n    20→\n    21→%%\n    22→\n    23→-module(ssh).\n    24→-moduledoc \"\"\"\n    25→Main API of the ssh application\n    26→\n    27→This is the interface module for the `SSH` application. The Secure Shell (SSH)\n    28→Protocol is a protocol for secure remote login and other secure network services\n    29→over an insecure network. See [ssh](ssh_app.md#supported) for details of\n    30→supported RFCs, versions, algorithms and unicode handling.\n    31→\n    32→With the SSH application it is possible to start _clients_ and to start\n    33→_daemons_ (servers).\n    34→\n    35→Clients are started with `connect/2`, `connect/3` or `connect/4`. They open an\n    36→encrypted connection on top of TCP/IP. In that encrypted connection one or more\n    37→channels could be opened with\n    38→[ssh_connection:session_channel/2,4](`ssh_connection:session_channel/2`).\n    39→\n    40→Each channel is an isolated \"pipe\" between a client-side process and a\n    41→server-side process. Those process pairs could handle for example file transfers\n    42→(sftp) or remote command execution (shell, exec and/or cli). If a custom shell\n    43→is implemented, the user of the client could execute the special commands\n    44→remotely. Note that the user is not necessarily a human but probably a system\n    45→interfacing the SSH app.\n    46→\n    47→A server-side subssystem (channel) server is requested by the client with\n    48→`ssh_connection:subsystem/4`.\n    49→\n    50→A server (daemon) is started with [daemon/1](`daemon/2`), `daemon/2` or\n    51→[daemon/3](`daemon/2`). Possible channel handlers (subsystems) are declared with\n    52→the [subsystem](`t:subsystem_daemon_option/0`) option when the daemon is\n    53→started.\n    54→\n    55→To just run a shell on a remote machine, there are functions that bundles the\n    56→needed three steps needed into one: [shell/1,2,3](`shell/1`). Similarly, to just\n    57→open an sftp (file transfer) connection to a remote machine, the simplest way is\n    58→to use [ssh_sftp:start_channel/1,2,3](`ssh_sftp:start_channel/1`).\n    59→\n    60→To write your own client channel handler, use the behaviour\n    61→`m:ssh_client_channel`. For server channel handlers use `m:ssh_server_channel`\n    62→behaviour (replaces ssh_daemon_channel).\n    63→\n    64→Both clients and daemons accept options that control the exact behaviour. Some\n    65→options are common to both. The three sets are called\n    66→[Client Options](`t:client_options/0`), [Daemon Options](`t:daemon_options/0`)\n    67→and [Common Options](`t:common_options/0`).\n    68→\n    69→The descriptions of the options uses the\n    70→[Erlang Type Language](`e:system:typespec.md`) with explaining text.\n    71→\n    72→> #### Note {: .info }\n    73→>\n    74→> See also [SSH Application Reference](index.html) and [Examples](using_ssh.md) section.\n    75→\n    76→## Keys and files\n    77→\n    78→A number of objects must be present for the SSH application to work. Those\n    79→objects are per default stored in files. The default names, paths and file\n    80→formats are the same as for [OpenSSH](http://www.openssh.com). Keys could be\n    81→generated with the `ssh-keygen` program from OpenSSH. See the\n    82→[User's Guide](using_ssh.md#running-an-erlang-ssh-daemon).\n    83→\n    84→The paths could easily be changed by options:\n    85→[`user_dir`](`t:ssh_file:user_dir_common_option/0`) and\n    86→[`system_dir`](`t:ssh_file:system_dir_daemon_option/0`).\n    87→\n    88→A completely different storage could be interfaced by writing callback modules\n    89→using the behaviours `m:ssh_client_key_api` and/or `m:ssh_server_key_api`. A\n    90→callback module is installed with the option\n    91→[`key_cb`](`t:key_cb_common_option/0`) to the client and/or the daemon.\n    92→\n    93→### Daemons\n    94→\n    95→The keys are by default stored in files:\n    96→\n    97→- Mandatory: one or more _Host key(s)_, both private and public. 80→sort_select_mthds1(Clients, Users0, Servers0) ->\n    81→    Servers = unique(Servers0),\n    82→    Users = unique(Users0),\n    83→    [C || Key <- Users,\n    84→\t  lists:member(Key, Servers),\n    85→\t  C <- Clients,\n    86→\t  element(1,C) == Key].\n    87→\n    88→unique(L) -> \n    89→    lists:reverse(\n    90→      lists:foldl(fun(E,Acc) -> \n    91→\t\t\t  case lists:member(E,Acc) of\n    92→\t\t\t      true -> Acc;\n    93→\t\t\t      false -> [E|Acc]\n    94→\t\t\t  end\n    95→\t\t  end, [], L)).\n    96→    \n    97→\n    98→%%%---- userauth_request_msg \"callbacks\"\n    99→password_msg([#ssh{opts = Opts,\n   100→\t\t   user = User,\n   101→                   service = Service} = Ssh0]) ->\n   102→    IoCb = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(io_cb, Opts),\n   103→    {Password,Ssh} = \n   104→\tcase ?GET_OPT(password, Opts) of\n   105→\t    undefined when IoCb == ssh_no_io ->\n   106→\t\t{not_ok, Ssh0};\n   107→\t    undefined -> \n   108→\t\t{IoCb:read_password(\"ssh password: \",Opts), Ssh0};\n   109→\t    PW ->\n   110→\t\t%% If \"password\" option is given it should not be tried again\n   111→\t\t{PW, Ssh0#ssh{opts = ?PUT_OPT({password,not_ok}, Opts)}}\n   112→\tend,\n   113→    case Password of\n   114→\tnot_ok ->\n   115→\t    {not_ok, Ssh};\n   116→\t_  ->\n   117→            {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n   118→                                       service = Service,\n   119→                                       method = \"password\",\n   120→                                       data =\n   121→                                           <<?BOOLEAN(?FALSE),\n   122→                                             ?STRING(unicode:characters_to_binary(Password))>>},\n   123→             Ssh}\n   124→    end.\n   125→\n   126→%% See RFC 4256 for info on keyboard-interactive\n   127→keyboard_interactive_msg([#ssh{user = User,\n   128→\t\t\t       opts = Opts,\n   129→\t\t\t       service = Service} = Ssh]) ->\n   130→    case ?GET_OPT(password, Opts) of\n   131→\tnot_ok ->\n   132→\t    {not_ok,Ssh};       % No need to use a failed pwd once more\n   133→\t_ ->\n   134→            {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n   135→                                       service = Service,\n   136→                                       method = \"keyboard-interactive\",\n   137→                                       data = << ?STRING(<<\"\">>),\n   138→                                                 ?STRING(<<>>) >> },\n   139→             Ssh}\n   140→    end.\n   141→\n   142→\n   143→get_public_key(SigAlg, #ssh{opts = Opts}) ->\n   144→    KeyAlg = key_alg(SigAlg),\n   145→    case ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(user_key, [KeyAlg], Opts) of\n   146→        {ok, {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob}} ->\n   147→            {ok, {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob}};\n   148→\n   149→        {ok, PrivKey} ->\n   150→            try\n   151→                %% Check the key - the KeyCb may be a buggy plugin\n   152→                true = ssh_transport:valid_key_sha_alg(private, PrivKey, KeyAlg),\n   153→                Key = ssh_file:extract_public_key(PrivKey),\n   154→                ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_encode(Key)\n   155→            of\n   156→                PubKeyBlob -> {ok, {PrivKey, PubKeyBlob}}\n   157→            catch\n   158→                _:_ -> \n   159→                    not_ok\n   160→            end;\n   161→\n   162→        _Error ->\n   163→            not_ok\n   164→    end.\n   165→\n   166→\n   167→publickey_msg([SigAlg, #ssh{user = User,\n   168→                            session_id = SessionId,\n   169→                            service = Service,\n   170→                            opts = Opts} = Ssh]) ->\n   171→    case get_public_key(SigAlg, Ssh) of\n   172→        {ok, {_, PubKeyBlob} = Key} ->\n   173→            SigAlgStr = atom_to_list(SigAlg),\n   174→            SigData = build_sig_data(SessionId, User, Service, PubKeyBlob, SigAlgStr),\n   175→\n   176→            SigRes = case Key of\n   177→                         {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob} ->\n   178→                             {ok, ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(sign, [PubKeyBlob, SigData], Opts)};\n   179→                         {PrivKey, PubKeyBlob} ->\n   180→                             ssh_transport:sign(SigData, SigAlg, PrivKey, Ssh)\n   181→                     end,\n   182→            case SigRes of\n   183→                {ok,Sig} ->\n   184→                    SigBlob = list_to_binary([?string(SigAlgStr),\n   185→                                              ?binary(Sig)]),\n   186→\n   187→                    {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n   188→                                               service = Service,\n   189→                                               method = \"publickey\",\n   190→                                               data = [?TRUE,\n   191→                                                       ?string(SigAlgStr),\n   192→                                                       ?binary(PubKeyBlob),\n   193→                                                       ?binary(SigBlob)]},\n   194→                     Ssh};\n   195→                {error,_} ->\n   196→                    {not_ok, Ssh}\n   197→            end;\n   198→\n   199→        _ ->\n   200→            {not_ok, Ssh}\n   201→    end.\n   202→\n   203→%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n   204→service_request_msg(Ssh) ->\n   205→    {#ssh_msg_service_request{name = \"ssh-userauth\"},\n   206→     Ssh#ssh{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}}.\n   207→\n   208→%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n   209→init_userauth_request_msg(#ssh{opts = Opts} = Ssh) ->\n   210→    %% Client side\n   211→    case ?GET_OPT(user, Opts) of\n   212→\tundefined ->\n   213→\t   248→                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh1}\n   249→            }\n   250→    end;\n   251→\n   252→handle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n 273→handle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n   274→\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n   275→\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"none\"}, _,\n                             partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n   421→    }.\n   422→\n   423→\n   424→%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n   425→%%% keyboard-interactive client\n   426→handle_userauth_info_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{name = Name,\n   427→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t    instruction = Instr,\n   428→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t    num_prompts = NumPrompts,\n 442→%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n   443→%%% keyboard-interactive server\n   444→handle_userauth_info_response(#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{num_responses = 477→handle_userauth_info_response({extra,#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{}},\n   478→\t\t\t      #ssh{user = User} = Ssh) ->\n   479→    {authorized, User,\n   480→     {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{},      \"Server does not support keyboard-interactive\").\n   486→\n   487→%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n   488→%%% Internal functions\n   489→%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n   490→method_preference(SigKeyAlgs) ->\n   491→    %% PubKeyAlgs: List of user (client) public key algorithms to try to use.\n   492→    %% All of the acceptable algorithms is the default values.\n   493→    PubKeyDefs = [{\"publickey\", ?MODULE, publickey_msg, [A]} || A <- SigKeyAlgs],\n   494→    NonPKmethods = [{\"password\", ?MODULE, password_msg, []},\n   495→                    {\"keyboard-interactive\", ?MODULE, keyboard_interactive_msg, []}\n   496→                   ],\n   497→    PubKeyDefs ++ NonPKmethods.\n   498→\n   499→check_user(User, Ssh) ->\n   500→    case ?GET_OPT(pk_check_user, Ssh#ssh.opts) of\n   501→        true ->\n   502→            check_password(User, pubkey, Ssh);\n   503→        _ ->\n   504→            {true, Ssh} % i.e, skip the test\n   505→    end.\n   506→\n   507→check_password(User, Password, #ssh{opts=Opts} = Ssh) ->\n   508→    case ?GET_OPT(pwdfun, Opts) of\n   509→        undefined when Password==pubkey ->\n   510→            %% Just check the User name\n   511→            case lists:keysearch(User, 1, ?GET_OPT(user_passwords,Opts)) of\n   512→                {value, {User, _}} -> {true, Ssh};\n   513→                false -> {false, Ssh}\n   514→            end;\n   515→\n   516→\tundefined ->\n   517→\t    Static = get_password_option(Opts, User),\n   518→\t    {ssh_lib:comp(Password,Static), Ssh};\n   519→\n   520→\tChecker when is_function(Checker,2) ->\n   521→\t    {Checker(User, Password), Ssh};\n   522→\n   523→\tChecker when is_function(Checker,4) ->\n   524→\t    #ssh{pwdfun_user_state = PrivateState,\n   525→\t\t peer = {_,PeerAddr={_,_}}\n   526→\t\t} = Ssh,\n   527→\t    case Checker(User, Password, PeerAddr, PrivateState) of\n   528→\t\ttrue ->\n   529→\t\t    {true,Ssh};\n   530→\t\tfalse ->\n   531→\t\t    {false,Ssh};\n   532→\t\t{true,NewState} ->\n   533→\t\t    {true, Ssh#ssh{pwdfun_user_state=NewState}};\n   534→\t\t{false,NewState} ->\n   535→\t\t    {false, Ssh#ssh{pwdfun_user_state=NewState}};\n   536→\t\tdisconnect ->\n   537→\t\t    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_NO_MORE_AUTH_METHODS_AVAILABLE,\n   538→                                \"\")\n   539→\t    end\n   540→    end.\n   541→\n   542→get_password_option(Opts, User) ->\n   543→    Passwords = ?GET_OPT(user_passwords, Opts),\n   544→    case lists:keysearch(User, 1, Passwords) of\n   545→\t{value, {User, Pw}} -> Pw;\n   546→\tfalse -> ?GET_OPT(password, Opts)\n   547→    end.\n   548→\t    \n   549→pre_verify_sig(User, KeyBlob,  #ssh{opts=Opts}) ->\n   550→    try\n   551→\tKey = ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_decode(KeyBlob), % or exception\n   552→        ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(is_auth_key, [Key, User], Opts)\n   553→    catch\n   554→\t_:_ ->\n   555→\t    false\n   556→    end.\n   557→\n   558→verify_sig(SessionId, User, Service, AlgBin, KeyBlob, SigWLen, #ssh{opts=Opts} = Ssh) ->\n   559→    try\n   560→        Alg = binary_to_list(AlgBin),\n   561→        true = lists:member(list_to_existing_atom(Alg), \n   562→                            proplists:get_value(public_key,\n   563→                                                ?GET_OPT(preferred_algorithms,Opts))),\n   564→        Key = ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_decode(KeyBlob), % or <<?UINT32(AlgSigLen), AlgSig:AlgSigLen/binary>> = SigWLen,\n   568→        <<?UINT32(AlgLen), _Alg:AlgLen/binary,\n   569→          ?UINT32(SigLen), Sig:SigLen/binary>> = AlgSig,\n   570→        ssh_transport:verify(PlainText, list_to_existing_atom(Alg), Sig, Key, Ssh)\n   571→    catch\n   572→\t_:_ ->\n   573→\t    false\n   574→    end.\n   575→\n   576→build_sig_data(SessionId, User, Service, KeyBlob, Alg) ->\n   577→    Sig = [?binary(SessionId),\n   578→\t   ?SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST,\n   579→\t   ?string_utf8(User),\n   580→\t   ?string(Service),\n   581→\t   ?binary(<<\"publickey\">>),\n   582→\t   ?TRUE,\n   583→\t   ?string(Alg),\n   584→\t   ?binary(KeyBlob)],\n   585→    list_to_binary(Sig).\n   586→\n   587→\n   588→\n   589→key_alg('rsa-sha2-256') -> 'ssh-rsa';\n   590→key_alg('rsa-sha2-512') -> 'ssh-rsa';\n   591→key_alg(Alg) -> Alg.\n   592→\n   593→%%%================================================================\n   594→%%%\n   595→%%% Keyboard-interactive\n   596→%%% \n   597→\n   598→decode_keyboard_interactive_prompts(_NumPrompts, Data) ->\n   599→    ssh_message:decode_keyboard_interactive_prompts(Data, []).\n   600→\n   601→keyboard_interact_get_responses(Opts, Name, Instr, PromptInfos) ->\n   602→    keyboard_interact_get_responses(?GET_OPT(user_interaction, Opts),\n   603→\t\t\t\t    ?GET_OPT(keyboard_interact_fun, Opts),\n   604→\t\t\t\t    ?GET_OPT(password, Opts),\n   605→                                    Name,\n   606→\t\t\t\t    Instr,\n   607→                                    PromptInfos,\n   608→                                    Opts).\n   609→\n   610→\n   611→%% Don't re-try an already rejected password. This could happen if both keyboard-interactive\n   612→%% and password methods are tried:\n   613→keyboard_interact_get_responses(_, _, not_ok, _, _, _, _) ->\n   614→    not_ok;\n   615→\n   616→%% Only one password requestedm and we have got one via the 'password' option for the daemon:\n   617→keyboard_interact_get_responses(_, undefined, Pwd, _, _, [_], _) when Pwd =/= undefined ->\n   618→    [Pwd]; %% Password auth implemented with keyboard-interaction and passwd is known\n   619→\n   620→%% No password requested (keyboard-interactive):\n   621→keyboard_interact_get_responses(_, _, _, _, _, [], _)  ->\n   622→    [];\n   623→\n   624→%% user_interaction is forbidden (by option user_interaction) and we have to ask\n   625→%% the user for one or more.\n   626→%% Throw an error:\n   627→keyboard_interact_get_responses(false, undefined, undefined, _, _, [Prompt|_], Opts) ->\n   628→    ssh_no_io:read_line(Prompt, Opts);\n   629→\n   630→%% One or more passwords are requested, we may prompt the user and no fun is used\n   631→%% to get the responses:\n   632→keyboard_interact_get_responses(true, undefined, _, Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts) ->\n   633→    prompt_user_for_passwords(Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts);\n   634→\n   635→%% The passwords are provided with a fun. Use that one!\n   636→keyboard_interact_get_responses(true, Fun, _Pwd, Name, Instr, PromptInfos, _Opts) ->\n   637→    keyboard_interact_fun(Fun, Name, Instr, PromptInfos).\n   638→\n   639→\n   640→\n   641→prompt_user_for_passwords(Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts) ->\n   642→    IoCb = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(io_cb, Opts),\n   643→    write_if_nonempty(IoCb, Name),\n   644→ 648→\t      PromptInfos).\n   649→\n   650→keyboard_interact_fun(KbdInteractFun, Name, Instr,  PromptInfos) ->\n   651→    case KbdInteractFun(Name, Instr, PromptInfos) of\n   652→\tResponses when is_list(Responses),\n   653→                     length(Responses) == length(PromptInfos) ->\n   654→\t    Responses;\n   655→\t_ ->\n   656→            nok\n   657→    end.\n   658→\n   659→\n   660→write_if_nonempty(_, \"\") -> ok;\n   661→write_if_nonempty(_, <<>>) -> ok;\n   662→write_if_nonempty(IoCb, Text) -> IoCb:format(\"~s~n\",[Text]).\n   663→\n         publickey_msg/1, password_msg/1, keyboard_interactive_msg/1,\n\t service_request_msg/1, init_userauth_request_msg/1,\n\t userauth_request_msg/1, handle_userauth_request/3, API\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\nuserauth_request_msg(#ssh{userauth_methods = ServerMethods,\n\t\t\t  userauth_supported_methods = UserPrefMethods,\n\t\t\t  userauth_preference = ClientMethods0\n\t\t\t } = Ssh0) ->\n    case sort_select_mthds(ClientMethods0, UserPrefMethods, ServerMethods) of\n\t[] ->\n            {send_disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_NO_MORE_AUTH_METHODS_AVAILABLE, Ssh0};\n\t\n\t[{Pref,Module,Function,Args} | Prefs] ->\n\t    Ssh = case Pref of\n\t\t      \"keyboard-interactive\" -> Ssh0;\n\t\t      _ -> Ssh0#ssh{userauth_preference = Prefs}\n\t\t  end,\n\t    case Module:Function(Args ++ [Ssh]) of\n\t\t{not_ok, Ssh1} ->\n\t\t    userauth_request_msg(Ssh1#ssh{userauth_preference = Prefs});\n\t\tResult ->\n\t\t    {Pref,Result}\n\t    end\n    end.\n\n\n\nsort_select_mthds(Clients, undefined, Servers) ->\n    %% User has not expressed an opinion via option \"auth_methods\", use the server's prefs\n    sort_select_mthds1(Clients, Servers, string:tokens(?SUPPORTED_AUTH_METHODS,\",\"));\n\nsort_select_mthds(Clients, Users0, Servers0) ->\n    %% The User has an opinion, use the intersection of that and the Servers whishes but\n    %% in the Users order\n    sort_select_mthds1(Clients, string:tokens(Users0,\",\"), Servers0).\n\n\nsort_select_mthds1(Clients, Users0, Servers0) ->\n    Servers = unique(Servers0),\n    Users = unique(Users0),\n    [C || Key <- Users,\n\t  lists:member(Key, Servers),\n\t  C <- Clients,\n\t  element(1,C) == Key].\n\nunique(L) -> \n    lists:reverse(\n      lists:foldl(fun(E,Acc) -> \n\t\t\t  case lists:member(E,Acc) of\n\t\t\t      true -> Acc;\n\t\t\t      false -> [E|Acc]\n\t\t\t  end\n\t\t  end, [], L)).\n    \n\n%%%---- userauth_request_msg \"callbacks\"\npassword_msg([#ssh{opts = Opts,\n\t\t   user = User,\n                   service = Service} = Ssh0]) ->\n    IoCb = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(io_cb, Opts),\n    {Password,Ssh} = \n\tcase ?GET_OPT(password, Opts) of\n\t    undefined when IoCb == ssh_no_io ->\n\t\t{not_ok, Ssh0};\n\t    undefined -> \n\t\t{IoCb:read_password(\"ssh password: \",Opts), Ssh0};\n\t    PW ->\n\t\t%% If \"password\" option is given it should not be tried again\n\t\t{PW, Ssh0#ssh{opts = ?PUT_OPT({password,not_ok}, Opts)}}\n\tend,\n    case Password of\n\tnot_ok ->\n\t    {not_ok, Ssh};\n\t_  ->\n            {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                       service = Service,\n                                       method = \"password\",\n                                       data =\n                                           <<?BOOLEAN(?FALSE),\n                                             ?STRING(unicode:characters_to_binary(Password))>>},\n             Ssh}\n    end.\n\n%% See RFC 4256 for info on keyboard-interactive\nkeyboard_interactive_msg([#ssh{user = User,\n\t\t\t       opts = Opts,\n\t\t\t       service = Service} = Ssh]) ->\n    case ?GET_OPT(password, Opts) of\n\tnot_ok ->\n\t    {not_ok,Ssh};       % No need to use a failed pwd once more\n\t_ ->\n            {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                       service = Service,\n                                       method = \"keyboard-interactive\",\n                                       data = << ?STRING(<<\"\">>),\n                                                 ?STRING(<<>>) >> },\n             Ssh}\n    end.\n\n\nget_public_key(SigAlg, #ssh{opts = Opts}) ->\n    KeyAlg = key_alg(SigAlg),\n    case ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(user_key, [KeyAlg], Opts) of\n        {ok, {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob}} ->\n            {ok, {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob}};\n\n        {ok, PrivKey} ->\n            try\n                %% Check the key - the KeyCb may be a buggy plugin\n                true = ssh_transport:valid_key_sha_alg(private, PrivKey, KeyAlg),\n                Key = ssh_file:extract_public_key(PrivKey),\n                ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_encode(Key)\n            of\n                PubKeyBlob -> {ok, {PrivKey, PubKeyBlob}}\n            catch\n                _:_ -> \n                    not_ok\n            end;\n\n        _Error ->\n            not_ok\n    end.\n\n\npublickey_msg([SigAlg, #ssh{user = User,\n                            session_id = SessionId,\n                            service = Service,\n                            opts = Opts} = Ssh]) ->\n    case get_public_key(SigAlg, Ssh) of\n        {ok, {_, PubKeyBlob} = Key} ->\n            SigAlgStr = atom_to_list(SigAlg),\n            SigData = build_sig_data(SessionId, User, Service, PubKeyBlob, SigAlgStr),\n\n            SigRes = case Key of\n                         {ssh2_pubkey, PubKeyBlob} ->\n                             {ok, ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(sign, [PubKeyBlob, SigData], Opts)};\n                         {PrivKey, PubKeyBlob} ->\n                             ssh_transport:sign(SigData, SigAlg, PrivKey, Ssh)\n                     end,\n            case SigRes of\n                {ok,Sig} ->\n                    SigBlob = list_to_binary([?string(SigAlgStr),\n                                              ?binary(Sig)]),\n\n                    {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                               service = Service,\n                                               method = \"publickey\",\n                                               data = [?TRUE,\n                                                       ?string(SigAlgStr),\n                                                       ?binary(PubKeyBlob),\n                                                       ?binary(SigBlob)]},\n                     Ssh};\n                {error,_} ->\n                    {not_ok, Ssh}\n            end;\n\n        _ ->\n            {not_ok, Ssh}\n    end.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\nservice_request_msg(Ssh) ->\n    {#ssh_msg_service_request{name = \"ssh-userauth\"},\n     Ssh#ssh{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}}.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\ninit_userauth_request_msg(#ssh{opts = Opts} = Ssh) ->\n    %% Client side\n    case ?GET_OPT(user, Opts) of\n\tundefined ->\n\t    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_ILLEGAL_USER_NAME,\n                        \"Could not determine the users name\");\n\tUser ->\n            {#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                       service = \"ssh-connection\",\n                                       method = \"none\",\n                                       data = <<>>},\n             Ssh#ssh{user = User,\n                     userauth_preference = method_preference(Ssh#ssh.userauth_pubkeys),\n                     userauth_methods = none,\n                     service = \"ssh-connection\"}\n            }\n    end.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% called by server\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_service_request{name = Name = \"ssh-userauth\"},\n\t\t\t_, Ssh) ->\n    {ok, {#ssh_msg_service_accept{name = Name},\n          Ssh#ssh{service = \"ssh-connection\"}}};\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"password\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  data = <<?FALSE, ?UINT32(Sz), BinPwd:Sz/binary>>}, _, \n\t\t\t#ssh{userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh) ->\n    Password = unicode:characters_to_list(BinPwd),\n    case check_password(User, Password, Ssh) of\n\t{true,Ssh1} ->\n\t    {authorized, User,\n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, Ssh1}\n            };\n\t{false,Ssh1}  ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, {error,\"Bad user or password\"}}, \n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh1}\n            }\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"password\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  data = <<?TRUE,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   _/binary\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   %% ?UINT32(Sz1), OldBinPwd:Sz1/binary,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   %% ?UINT32(Sz2), NewBinPwd:Sz2/binary\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t >>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t }, _, \n\t\t\t#ssh{userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh) ->\n    %% Password change without us having sent SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_PASSWD_CHANGEREQ (because we never do)\n    %% RFC 4252 says:\n    %%   SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_FAILURE without partial success - The password\n    %%   has not been changed.  Either password changing was not supported,\n    %%   or the old password was bad. \n\n    {not_authorized, {User, {error,\"Password change not supported\"}}, \n     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n    };\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"none\"}, _,\n\t\t\t#ssh{userauth_supported_methods = Methods,\n                             opts = Opts} = Ssh) ->\n    case ?GET_OPT(no_auth_needed, Opts) of\n        false ->\n            %% The normal case\n            {not_authorized, {User, undefined},\n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n            };\n        true ->\n            %% RFC 4252  5.2\n\t    {authorized, User,\n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, Ssh}\n            }\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"publickey\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  data = <<?BYTE(?FALSE),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   ?UINT32(ALen), BAlg:ALen/binary,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   ?UINT32(KLen), KeyBlob:KLen/binary,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   _/binary\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t >>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t }, \n\t\t\t_SessionId, \n\t\t\t#ssh{userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh0) ->\n    Ssh =\n        case check_user(User, Ssh0) of\n            {true,Ssh01} -> Ssh01#ssh{user=User};\n            {false,Ssh01} -> Ssh01#ssh{user=false}\n        end,\n\n    case\n        pre_verify_sig(User, KeyBlob, Ssh)\n    of\n\ttrue ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, undefined},\n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_pk_ok{algorithm_name = binary_to_list(BAlg),\n                                     key_blob = KeyBlob}, Ssh}\n            };\n\tfalse ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, undefined}, \n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n            }\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"publickey\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  data = <<?BYTE(?TRUE),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   ?UINT32(ALen), BAlg:ALen/binary,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   ?UINT32(KLen), KeyBlob:KLen/binary,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   SigWLen/binary>>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t }, \n\t\t\tSessionId, \n\t\t\t#ssh{user = PreVerifyUser,\n                             userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh0) ->\n    \n    {UserOk,Ssh} = check_user(User, Ssh0),\n    case\n        ((PreVerifyUser == User) orelse (PreVerifyUser == undefined)) andalso\n        UserOk andalso\n        verify_sig(SessionId, User, \"ssh-connection\", BAlg, KeyBlob, SigWLen, Ssh)\n    of\n\ttrue ->\n\t    {authorized, User, \n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, Ssh}\n            };\n\tfalse ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, undefined}, \n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n            }\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = \"keyboard-interactive\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  data = _},\n\t\t\t_, #ssh{opts = Opts,\n\t\t\t\tkb_tries_left = KbTriesLeft,\n\t\t\t\tuserauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh) ->\n    case KbTriesLeft of\n\tN when N<1 ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, {authmethod, \"keyboard-interactive\"}}, \n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n            };\n\n\t_ ->\n\t    %% RFC4256\n\t    %% The data field contains:\n\t    %%   - language tag (deprecated). If =/=[] SHOULD use it however. We skip\n\t    %%                                it for simplicity.\n\t    %%   - submethods. \"... the user can give a hint of which actual methods\n\t    %%                  he wants to use. ...\".  It's a \"MAY use\" so we skip\n\t    %%                  it. It also needs an understanding between the client\n\t    %%                  and the server.\n\t    %%                  \n\t    %% \"The server MUST reply with an SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS,\n\t    %%  SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_FAILURE, or SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_INFO_REQUEST message.\"\n\t    Default = {\"SSH server\",\n\t\t       \"Enter password for \\\"\"++User++\"\\\"\",\n\t\t       \"password: \",\n\t\t       false},\n\n\t    {Name, Instruction, Prompt, Echo} =\n\t\tcase ?GET_OPT(auth_method_kb_interactive_data, Opts) of\n\t\t    undefined -> \n\t\t\tDefault;\n\t\t    {_,_,_,_}=V -> \n\t\t\tV;\n                    F when is_function(F, 4) ->\n\t\t\t{_,PeerName} = Ssh#ssh.peer,\n\t\t\tF(PeerName, User, \"ssh-connection\", Ssh#ssh.pwdfun_user_state);\n\t\t    F when is_function(F) ->\n\t\t\t{_,PeerName} = Ssh#ssh.peer,\n\t\t\tF(PeerName, User, \"ssh-connection\")\n\t\tend,\n\t    EchoEnc = case Echo of\n\t\t\t  true -> <<?TRUE>>;\n\t\t\t  false -> <<?FALSE>>\n\t\t      end,\n\t    Msg = #ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{name = unicode:characters_to_list(Name),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t instruction = unicode:characters_to_list(Instruction),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t language_tag = \"\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t num_prompts = 1,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t data = <<?STRING(unicode:characters_to_binary(Prompt)),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t  EchoEnc/binary\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t>>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, undefined}, \n\t     {Msg, Ssh#ssh{user = User}}\n            }\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t  method = Other}, _,\n\t\t\t#ssh{userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh) ->\n    {not_authorized, {User, {authmethod, Other}}, \n     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                partial_success = false}, Ssh}\n    }.\n\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% keyboard-interactive client\nhandle_userauth_info_request(#ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{name = Name,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t    instruction = Instr,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t    num_prompts = NumPrompts,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t    data  = Data},\n\t\t\t     #ssh{opts=Opts} = Ssh) ->\n    PromptInfos = decode_keyboard_interactive_prompts(NumPrompts,Data),\n    case keyboard_interact_get_responses(Opts, Name, Instr, PromptInfos) of\n\tnot_ok ->\n\t    not_ok;\n\tResponses ->\n\t    {ok, \n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{num_responses = NumPrompts,\n                                              data = Responses},\n              Ssh}}\n    end.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% keyboard-interactive server\nhandle_userauth_info_response(#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{num_responses = 1,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t      data = <<?UINT32(Sz), Password:Sz/binary>>},\n\t\t\t      #ssh{opts = Opts,\n\t\t\t\t   kb_tries_left = KbTriesLeft,\n\t\t\t\t   user = User,\n\t\t\t\t   userauth_supported_methods = Methods} = Ssh) ->\n    SendOneEmpty =\n\t(?GET_OPT(tstflg,Opts) == one_empty)\n\torelse \n\tproplists:get_value(one_empty, ?GET_OPT(tstflg,Opts), false),\n\n    case check_password(User, unicode:characters_to_list(Password), Ssh) of\n\t{true,Ssh1} when SendOneEmpty==true ->\n\t    {authorized_but_one_more, User,\n             {#ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{name = \"\",\n                                             instruction = \"\",\n                                             language_tag = \"\",\n                                             num_prompts = 0,\n                                             data = <<?BOOLEAN(?FALSE)>>\n                                            },\n              Ssh1}};\n\n\t{true,Ssh1} ->\n\t    {authorized, User,\n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, Ssh1}};\n\n\t{false,Ssh1} ->\n\t    {not_authorized, {User, {error,\"Bad user or password\"}}, \n\t     {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{authentications = Methods,\n                                        partial_success = false}, \n              Ssh1#ssh{kb_tries_left = max(KbTriesLeft-1, 0)}}}\n    end;\n\nhandle_userauth_info_response({extra,#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{}},\n\t\t\t      #ssh{user = User} = Ssh) ->\n    {authorized, User,\n     {#ssh_msg_userauth_success{}, Ssh}};\n\nhandle_userauth_info_response(#ssh_msg_userauth_info_response{},\n\t\t\t      _Auth) ->\n    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE,\n                \"Server does not support keyboard-interactive\").\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Internal functions\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nmethod_preference(SigKeyAlgs) ->\n    %% PubKeyAlgs: List of user (client) public key algorithms to try to use.\n    %% All of the acceptable algorithms is the default values.\n    PubKeyDefs = [{\"publickey\", ?MODULE, publickey_msg, [A]} || A <- SigKeyAlgs],\n    NonPKmethods = [{\"password\", ?MODULE, password_msg, []},\n                    {\"keyboard-interactive\", ?MODULE, keyboard_interactive_msg, []}\n                   ],\n    PubKeyDefs ++ NonPKmethods.\n\ncheck_user(User, Ssh) ->\n    case ?GET_OPT(pk_check_user, Ssh#ssh.opts) of\n        true ->\n            check_password(User, pubkey, Ssh);\n        _ ->\n            {true, Ssh} % i.e, skip the test\n    end.\n\ncheck_password(User, Password, #ssh{opts=Opts} = Ssh) ->\n    case ?GET_OPT(pwdfun, Opts) of\n        undefined when Password==pubkey ->\n            %% Just check the User name\n            case lists:keysearch(User, 1, ?GET_OPT(user_passwords,Opts)) of\n                {value, {User, _}} -> {true, Ssh};\n                false -> {false, Ssh}\n            end;\n\n\tundefined ->\n\t    Static = get_password_option(Opts, User),\n\t    {ssh_lib:comp(Password,Static), Ssh};\n\n\tChecker when is_function(Checker,2) ->\n\t    {Checker(User, Password), Ssh};\n\n\tChecker when is_function(Checker,4) ->\n\t    #ssh{pwdfun_user_state = PrivateState,\n\t\t peer = {_,PeerAddr={_,_}}\n\t\t} = Ssh,\n\t    case Checker(User, Password, PeerAddr, PrivateState) of\n\t\ttrue ->\n\t\t    {true,Ssh};\n\t\tfalse ->\n\t\t    {false,Ssh};\n\t\t{true,NewState} ->\n\t\t    {true, Ssh#ssh{pwdfun_user_state=NewState}};\n\t\t{false,NewState} ->\n\t\t    {false, Ssh#ssh{pwdfun_user_state=NewState}};\n\t\tdisconnect ->\n\t\t    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_NO_MORE_AUTH_METHODS_AVAILABLE,\n                                \"\")\n\t    end\n    end.\n\nget_password_option(Opts, User) ->\n    Passwords = ?GET_OPT(user_passwords, Opts),\n    case lists:keysearch(User, 1, Passwords) of\n\t{value, {User, Pw}} -> Pw;\n\tfalse -> ?GET_OPT(password, Opts)\n    end.\n\t    \npre_verify_sig(User, KeyBlob,  #ssh{opts=Opts}) ->\n    try\n\tKey = ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_decode(KeyBlob), % or exception\n        ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(is_auth_key, [Key, User], Opts)\n    catch\n\t_:_ ->\n\t    false\n    end.\n\nverify_sig(SessionId, User, Service, AlgBin, KeyBlob, SigWLen, #ssh{opts=Opts} = Ssh) ->\n    try\n        Alg = binary_to_list(AlgBin),\n        true = lists:member(list_to_existing_atom(Alg), \n                            proplists:get_value(public_key,\n                                                ?GET_OPT(preferred_algorithms,Opts))),\n        Key = ssh_message:ssh2_pubkey_decode(KeyBlob), % or exception\n        true = ssh_transport:call_KeyCb(is_auth_key, [Key, User], Opts),\n        PlainText = build_sig_data(SessionId, User, Service, KeyBlob, Alg),\n        <<?UINT32(AlgSigLen), AlgSig:AlgSigLen/binary>> = SigWLen,\n        <<?UINT32(AlgLen), _Alg:AlgLen/binary,\n          ?UINT32(SigLen), Sig:SigLen/binary>> = AlgSig,\n        ssh_transport:verify(PlainText, list_to_existing_atom(Alg), Sig, Key, Ssh)\n    catch\n\t_:_ ->\n\t    false\n    end.\n\nbuild_sig_data(SessionId, User, Service, KeyBlob, Alg) ->\n    Sig = [?binary(SessionId),\n\t   ?SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST,\n\t   ?string_utf8(User),\n\t   ?string(Service),\n\t   ?binary(<<\"publickey\">>),\n\t   ?TRUE,\n\t   ?string(Alg),\n\t   ?binary(KeyBlob)],\n    list_to_binary(Sig).\n\n\n\nkey_alg('rsa-sha2-256') -> 'ssh-rsa';\nkey_alg('rsa-sha2-512') -> 'ssh-rsa';\nkey_alg(Alg) -> Alg.\n\n%%%================================================================\n%%%\n%%% Keyboard-interactive\n%%% \n\ndecode_keyboard_interactive_prompts(_NumPrompts, Data) ->\n    ssh_message:decode_keyboard_interactive_prompts(Data, []).\n\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(Opts, Name, Instr, PromptInfos) ->\n    keyboard_interact_get_responses(?GET_OPT(user_interaction, Opts),\n\t\t\t\t    ?GET_OPT(keyboard_interact_fun, Opts),\n\t\t\t\t    ?GET_OPT(password, Opts),\n                                    Name,\n\t\t\t\t    Instr,\n                                    PromptInfos,\n                                    Opts).\n\n\n%% Don't re-try an already rejected password. This could happen if both keyboard-interactive\n%% and password methods are tried:\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(_, _, not_ok, _, _, _, _) ->\n    not_ok;\n\n%% Only one password requestedm and we have got one via the 'password' option for the daemon:\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(_, undefined, Pwd, _, _, [_], _) when Pwd =/= undefined ->\n    [Pwd]; %% Password auth implemented with keyboard-interaction and passwd is known\n\n%% No password requested (keyboard-interactive):\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(_, _, _, _, _, [], _)  ->\n    [];\n\n%% user_interaction is forbidden (by option user_interaction) and we have to ask\n%% the user for one or more.\n%% Throw an error:\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(false, undefined, undefined, _, _, [Prompt|_], Opts) ->\n    ssh_no_io:read_line(Prompt, Opts);\n\n%% One or more passwords are requested, we may prompt the user and no fun is used\n%% to get the responses:\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(true, undefined, _, Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts) ->\n    prompt_user_for_passwords(Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts);\n\n%% The passwords are provided with a fun. Use that one!\nkeyboard_interact_get_responses(true, Fun, _Pwd, Name, Instr, PromptInfos, _Opts) ->\n    keyboard_interact_fun(Fun, Name, Instr, PromptInfos).\n\n\n\nprompt_user_for_passwords(Name, Instr, PromptInfos, Opts) ->\n    IoCb = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(io_cb, Opts),\n    write_if_nonempty(IoCb, Name),\n    write_if_nonempty(IoCb, Instr),\n    lists:map(fun({Prompt, true})  -> IoCb:read_line(Prompt, Opts);\n\t\t ({Prompt, false}) -> IoCb:read_password(Prompt, Opts)\n\t      end,\n\t      PromptInfos).\n\nkeyboard_interact_fun(KbdInteractFun, Name, Instr,  PromptInfos) ->\n    case KbdInteractFun(Name, Instr, PromptInfos) of\n\tResponses when is_list(Responses),\n                     length(Responses) == length(PromptInfos) ->\n\t    Responses;\n\t_ ->\n            nok\n    end.\n\n\nwrite_if_nonempty(_, \"\") -> ok;\nwrite_if_nonempty(_, <<>>) -> ok;\nwrite_if_nonempty(IoCb, Text) -> IoCb:format(\"~s~n\",[Text]).\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Called just for the tracer ssh_dbg\nssh_msg_userauth_result(_R) -> ok.\n\n%%%################################################################\n%%%#\n%%%# Tracing\n%%%#\n\nssh_dbg_trace_points() -> [authentication].\n\nssh_dbg_flags(authentication) -> [c].\n\nssh_dbg_on(authentication) -> dbg:tp(?MODULE, handle_userauth_request, 3, x),\n                              dbg:tp(?MODULE, init_userauth_request_msg, 1, x),\n                              dbg:tp(?MODULE, ssh_msg_userauth_result, 1, x),\n                              dbg:tp(?MODULE, userauth_request_msg, 1, x).\n\nssh_dbg_off(authentication) -> dbg:ctpg(?MODULE, handle_userauth_request, 3),\n                               dbg:ctpg(?MODULE, init_userauth_request_msg, 1),\n                               dbg:ctpg(?MODULE, ssh_msg_userauth_result, 1),\n                               dbg:ctpg(?MODULE, userauth_request_msg, 1).\n\n\n\n%%% Server ----------------\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {call, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request, [Req,_SessionID,Ssh]}},\n               Stack) ->\n    {skip, [{Req,Ssh}|Stack]};\n\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {ok,{#ssh_msg_service_accept{name=Name},_Ssh}}},\n               [{#ssh_msg_service_request{name=Name},_} | Stack]) ->\n    {skip, Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {authorized,User,_Repl}},\n              [{#ssh_msg_userauth_request{}=Req,Ssh}|Stack]) ->\n    {[\"AUTH srvr: Peer client authorized\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~n\", [User]),\n      fmt_req(Req, Ssh)],\n     Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {not_authorized,{User,_X},_Repl}},\n               [{#ssh_msg_userauth_request{method=\"none\"},Ssh}|Stack]) ->\n    Methods = Ssh#ssh.userauth_supported_methods,\n    {[\"AUTH srvr: Peer queries auth methods\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~nsupported methods = ~p ?\", [User,Methods])\n     ],\n     Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {not_authorized,{User,_X}, Repl}\n                               },\n              [{#ssh_msg_userauth_request{method = \"publickey\",\n                                          data = <<?BYTE(?FALSE), _/binary>>\n                                         }=Req,Ssh}|Stack]) ->\n    {case Repl of\n         {#ssh_msg_userauth_pk_ok{}, _} ->\n             [\"AUTH srvr: Answer - pub key supported\\n\"];\n          {#ssh_msg_userauth_failure{}, _} ->\n             [\"AUTH srvr: Answer - pub key not supported\\n\"];\n          {Other, _} ->\n             [\"AUTH srvr: Answer - strange answer\\n\",\n              io_lib:format(\"strange answer = ~p~n\",[Other])\n             ]\n      end\n     ++ [io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~n\", [User]),\n         fmt_req(Req, Ssh)],\n     Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {not_authorized,{User,_X},\n                                 {#ssh_msg_userauth_info_request{},_Ssh}}},\n               [{#ssh_msg_userauth_request{method=\"keyboard-interactive\"\n                                          } = Req,Ssh}|Stack]) ->\n    {[\"AUTH srvr: Ask peer client for password\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~n\", [User]),\n      fmt_req(Req, Ssh)],\n     Stack};\n\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {call, {?MODULE,ssh_msg_userauth_result,[success]}},\n               Stack) ->\n    {[\"AUTH client: Success\"],Stack};\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,ssh_msg_userauth_result,1}, _Result},\n               Stack) ->\n    {skip, Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,handle_userauth_request,3},\n                                {not_authorized,{User,_X},_Repl}},\n              [{#ssh_msg_userauth_request{}=Req,Ssh}|Stack]) ->\n    {[\"AUTH srvr: Peer client authorization failed\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~n\", [User]),\n      fmt_req(Req, Ssh)],\n     Stack};\n\n%%% Client ----------------\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {call, {?MODULE,init_userauth_request_msg, [#ssh{opts = Opts}]}},\n               Stack) ->\n    {[\"AUTH client: Service ssh-userauth accepted\\n\",\n      case ?GET_OPT(user, Opts) of\n          undefined ->\n              io_lib:format(\"user = undefined *** ERROR ***\", []);\n          User ->\n              io_lib:format(\"user = ~p\", [User])\n      end\n     ],\n     Stack};\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from, {?MODULE,init_userauth_request_msg,1},\n                                {Repl = #ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                                                  service = \"ssh-connection\",\n                                                                  method = \"none\"},\n                                 _Ssh}},\n               Stack) ->\n    {[\"AUTH client: Query for accepted methods\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p\", [User])],\n     [Repl|Stack]};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication,  {call, {?MODULE,userauth_request_msg,\n                                        [#ssh{userauth_methods = Methods}]}},\n               [ #ssh_msg_userauth_request{user = User,\n                                           service = \"ssh-connection\",\n                                           method = \"none\"} | Stack]) ->\n    {[\"AUTH client: Server supports\\n\",\n      io_lib:format(\"user = ~p~nmethods = ~p\", [User,Methods])],\n     Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication,  {call, {?MODULE,userauth_request_msg,[_Ssh]}},\n               Stack) ->\n    {skip,Stack};\n\nssh_dbg_format(authentication, {return_from,
